# Dunmar Frontier campaign timeline

Working chronology from the structured transcript records, starting March 22, 1748. Sessions 96, 107 and 135 have no structured coverage.

Watches: dawn 02–06 · morning 06–10 · midday 10–14 · afternoon 14–18 · dusk 18–22 · midnight 22–02. Unknown watches are omitted. “By” marks an uncertain date; its lower bound is preserved in the [data](timeline.json).

[Date discrepancies and coverage review](review.md) · [Combat index](combat-index.md) · [Structured timeline](timeline.json)

Level numbers and missing advancement milestones follow the [combat analysis](../../../_analysis/sessions/combat/metadata.yaml); “By” also marks awards known only by a session boundary.

- **March 22, 1748 · morning** — [S1](../dunmari-frontier-001/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-001-time-markers.json). Candrosa is the temple’s head; The temple’s public face and secret initiations.
- **March 22, 1748 · morning** — [S1](../dunmari-frontier-001/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-001-time-markers.json). **⚔ Combat DF001-C01 · level 1:** Giant hyenas attack Karawa.
- **March 22, 1748 · morning** — [S1](../dunmari-frontier-001/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-001-time-markers.json). Badya is entrusted with the injured villager; Cha’mutte and the devastation of Dunmar; Beli heals Delwath and Kenzo and takes the wounded man; Kisa asks for help; Candrosa reacts to the magical disturbance.
- **March 22, 1748 · morning** — [S1](../dunmari-frontier-001/cleaned/dunmari-frontier-001-time-markers.json). Kisa defends Karawa and Wellby apologizes; Candrosa invites a later meeting; Kisa entrusts the investigation; Kenzo obtains a room from the tavern owner; The temple interior remains secret.

- **November 3, 1749** — [S139](../dunmari-frontier-139/cleaned/dunmar-frontier-139-time-markers.json). The Boulder ladder becomes a memorial; The sixth Ladder is set free; The five predecessor ladders; Milo and Tilly reach a southern port.
